# 컷별 연출·조명·이미지 및 영상 프롬프트

렌즈·광원·시간·점수는 모두 재현 제안입니다. 첨부 이미지의 실제 촬영 수치가 아닙니다. 영상은 아직 생성하지 않았으며 이 문서는 이미지 제작과 향후 영상화 설계입니다.

## 001 · 00:00–00:05 · NORMAL

| 항목 | 설계 |
|---|---|
| 대본 | 오늘 아침 눈을 떴습니다. 그런데 휴대폰이 없습니다. 전등도 없습니다. |
| 목적 | 거친 삼베 침구 옆의 꺼진 청동 등잔과 덧문에서 들어오는 첫 햇빛을 극근접으로 보여준다. |
| 감정 | 낯섦·호기심 |
| Hero Object | 청동 등잔과 삼베 |
| 시작 구도 | CU 85mm 침상 측면 |
| 카메라 이동 | 등잔 표면에서 낯선 목재 천장 방향으로 시선 이동 |
| 전경 | 삼베 올 |
| 중경 | 청동 등잔 |
| 배경 | 빛나는 덧문 |
| Reveal | 거친 삼베 침구 옆의 꺼진 청동 등잔과 덧문에서 들어오는 첫 햇빛을 극근접으로 보여준다. |
| 인물 행동 | 인물은 규모 기준 또는 주변 생활 동작만; 주요 사물의 형태를 유지 |
| Sound | {"ambience": "멀리 도시·말발굽", "action": "청동 등잔과 삼베에 해당하는 실제 접촉·환경 소리만", "transition": "짧은 음향 브리지; 모든 컷에 whoosh 금지", "music": "대사 아래의 낮은 레벨, 발견 순간만 상승", "silence": "필요 없는 효과음 비움"} |
| 이전 컷 연결 | 시작: 암전에서 청동 등잔과 삼베의 질감으로 |
| 다음 컷 Anchor | #002 목재 들보 / CENTER_MIDDLE |

조명 — 모두 재현 제안:

- evidence: 재현 제안: 원본 촬영값이나 측정치 아님
- source: 동쪽 창·아침 태양
- direction: 카메라 왼쪽 위의 측후면
- contrast: 얼굴·기와의 왼쪽 테두리; 오른쪽은 푸른 주변광
- softness: 작은 창의 비교적 선명한 경계, 하늘 보조광은 부드러움
- color: 금빛 주광·청록 그림자, 피부 중립
- particles: 창 빛이 지나는 작은 구간에만 먼지
- 시간 변화: 실제 시작·중간·끝 프레임은 아직 없음. 영상화 시 광원을 고정하고 피사체·카메라·입자 움직임을 분리해 비교.
- 다음 컷: 같은 장소는 세계 좌표 광원 유지, 다른 장소는 편집 전환. velocity_in/out는 미측정.

기법 출처: C1, C2, L1 (04 문서 참조)

Image prompt:

```text
LATEST USER CINEMATOGRAPHY REQUIREMENT: a composed live-action historical MOVIE still with shot-specific cinematic camera placement, NOT a fixed first-person game view, NOT a repeated behind-avatar game camera, no game HUD. Wide oblique aerial, low-angle architecture, telephoto layers, dynamic diagonal composition, intimate object close-ups and macro details alternate across the sequence. Do not automatically insert the viewer's arms or hands. Human fragments belong only when the stated story action needs them. No face-forward speaking presenter, talking head or frontal face close-up. Featured people are beautifully costumed but viewed from behind, cropped to shoulder/torso/hands, or hidden behind a motivated foreground element. Make spectacular surrounding architecture, fabric, flora and daily activity abundant yet compositionally controlled. A purposeful object, garment detail, horse, bird or other explicitly specified anchor may guide a later cinematic transition; never insert animals indiscriminately. Natural depth through camera placement, overlapping planes, visual scale and lighting. Use case: historical-scene (scientific and modern-research inserts as explicitly specified). Asset type: ONE independent full-frame cinematic documentary still, landscape 16:9, target 1536x864 or the closest supported high-quality landscape resolution. Never a collage, storyboard sheet, contact sheet, poster, screenshot, caption card or multiple panels. Premium photographic realism with tangible linen, rope, timber, skin, brick, stone and metal. Beautifully abundant surroundings organized into three readable depth layers; a strong single focal subject, a clear travel corridor and visible human scale when architectural. Color inspiration: jewel-like teal and emerald accents, cinnabar and terracotta, Egyptian blue, luminous ivory and controlled bronze-gold highlights, deep dimensional shadows with recoverable texture. Preserve historically plausible Roman architecture and dress; interpretive reconstruction rather than pretending to be an ancient photograph. No copied source-image people, watermarks, brands, interface, lettering, subtitles, fake inscriptions, fantasy armor, baroque rococo interiors, electric lights, modern cranes, Aurelian walls or Arch of Constantine in AD 117. No excessive bloom, uniformly orange grading, flat gray ruins, plastic faces or indiscriminate fog. Still image, no motion blur on the focal subject. Lens values are compositional intent, not claims of real camera metadata.
Shot 001. Scene: Cinematic 85mm close-up from beside a Roman straw bed: coarse linen folds fill the near lower foreground, a small exquisitely worked but plausible bronze oil lamp rests unlit on a weathered stool, and sunlight from wooden shutters creates glowing bronze edges and long cool shadows. No person, hands or body-point-of-view framing. Three-dimensional material contrast, shallow but intelligible room depth, evocative waking atmosphere. 
Composition: CU 85mm 침상 측면. Foreground: 삼베 올. Midground: 청동 등잔. Background: 빛나는 덧문. Hero subject: 청동 등잔과 삼베, clearly readable near the center third. Depth comes from actual near/far camera placement, perspective, occlusion, scale and atmospheric separation, never merely digital zoom. warm morning sunlight from upper camera-left, cooler sky fill, bright edges and rich open shadows. Reserve a coherent central or near-central travel path. Image must stand alone with lush but purposeful surrounding detail.
```

Video prompt — 아직 실행하지 않은 후속 설계:

```text
별도 후속 영상화 제안 / 5초 편집 구간. 등잔 표면에서 낯선 목재 천장 방향으로 시선 이동. 0~0.4초 시작 공간을 읽게 하고, 약 2.2초 주요 대상 도달, 3.8초 거친 삼베 침구 옆의 꺼진 청동 등잔과 덧문에서 들어오는 첫 햇빛을 극근접으로 보여준다.의 정보를 읽게 함. 마지막 0.5초 다음 앵커 #002 목재 들보 / CENTER_MIDDLE. 일반 컷 주이동 1개·방향전환 최대 1회; 강한 컷도 원인 있는 이동만. 건축물 고정, 손과 접촉·발 위치 유지. 실제 이전 영상에서 마지막 사용 프레임과 관측 속도가 확보되는 경우에만 첫 0.3~0.5초 그 관성 유지. 현재는 실제 프레임 연결이 검증되지 않은 독립 이미지다. 물질 내부·지도·시대 전환은 편집/설명 화면 전환이며 실제 관통 촬영이 아니다.
```

별도 후속 영상화 제안 / 4초 편집 구간. 열린 창까지만 전진. 0~0.4초 시작 공간을 읽게 하고, 약 1.8초 주요 대상 도달, 3.0초 손이 목재 덧문을 열자 황금빛 도시가 틈으로 보인다.의 정보를 읽게 함. 마지막 0.5초 다음 앵커 #004 기와지붕 / CENTER_MIDDLE. 일반 컷 주이동 1개·방향전환 최대 1회; 강한 컷도 원인 있는 이동만. 건축물 고정, 손과 접촉·발 위치 유지. 실제 이전 영상에서 마지막 사용 프레임과 관측 속도가 확보되는 경우에만 첫 0.3~0.5초 그 관성 유지. 현재는 실제 프레임 연결이 검증되지 않은 독립 이미지다. 물질 내부·지도·시대 전환은 편집/설명 화면 전환이며 실제 관통 촬영이 아니다.
```

Image prompt:

```text
LATEST USER CINEMATOGRAPHY REQUIREMENT: a composed live-action historical MOVIE still with shot-specific cinematic camera placement, NOT a fixed first-person game view, NOT a repeated behind-avatar game camera, no game HUD. Wide oblique aerial, low-angle architecture, telephoto layers, dynamic diagonal composition, intimate object close-ups and macro details alternate across the sequence. Do not automatically insert the viewer's arms or hands. Human fragments belong only when the stated story action needs them. No face-forward speaking presenter, talking head or frontal face close-up. Featured people are beautifully costumed but viewed from behind, cropped to shoulder/torso/hands, or hidden behind a motivated foreground element. Make spectacular surrounding architecture, fabric, flora and daily activity abundant yet compositionally controlled. A purposeful object, garment detail, horse, bird or other explicitly specified anchor may guide a later cinematic transition; never insert animals indiscriminately. Natural depth through camera placement, overlapping planes, visual scale and lighting. Use case: historical-scene (scientific and modern-research inserts as explicitly specified). Asset type: ONE independent full-frame cinematic documentary still, landscape 16:9, target 1536x864 or the closest supported high-quality landscape resolution. Never a collage, storyboard sheet, contact sheet, poster, screenshot, caption card or multiple panels. Premium photographic realism with tangible linen, rope, timber, skin, brick, stone and metal. Beautifully abundant surroundings organized into three readable depth layers; a strong single focal subject, a clear travel corridor and visible human scale when architectural. Color inspiration: jewel-like teal and emerald accents, cinnabar and terracotta, Egyptian blue, luminous ivory and controlled bronze-gold highlights, deep dimensional shadows with recoverable texture. Preserve historically plausible Roman architecture and dress; interpretive reconstruction rather than pretending to be an ancient photograph. No copied source-image people, watermarks, brands, interface, lettering, subtitles, fake inscriptions, fantasy armor, baroque rococo interiors, electric lights, modern cranes, Aurelian walls or Arch of Constantine in AD 117. No excessive bloom, uniformly orange grading, flat gray ruins, plastic faces or indiscriminate fog. Still image, no motion blur on the focal subject. Lens values are compositional intent, not claims of real camera metadata.
Shot 004. Scene: Roof-level view of thriving ancient Rome AD 117, close terracotta roof edges, densely layered plausible Roman housing, richly painted temples and Trajan's baths, the Colosseum distant and correctly oval, golden sunrise through dramatic clouds, monumental but geographically credible. A small swift near the close roof edge points toward the distant amphitheater and provides an optional later transition anchor; the bird never dominates the city.
Composition: EW 18mm 지붕 높이. Foreground: 기와. Midground: 주택군. Background: 콜로세움. Hero subject: 기와지붕, clearly readable near the center third. Depth comes from actual near/far camera placement, perspective, occlusion, scale and atmospheric separation, never merely digital zoom. warm morning sunlight from upper camera-left, cooler sky fill, bright edges and rich open shadows. Reserve a coherent central or near-central travel path. Image must stand alone with lush but purposeful surrounding detail.
```

별도 후속 영상화 제안 / 7초 편집 구간. 횡이동으로 서로 다른 거리 흐름 공개. 0~0.4초 시작 공간을 읽게 하고, 약 3.1초 주요 대상 도달, 5.2초 골목과 대로의 군중이 수평선까지 이어져 인구 규모를 체감시킨다.의 정보를 읽게 함. 마지막 0.5초 다음 앵커 #007 거친 소매와 빈 허리띠 / CENTER_MIDDLE. 일반 컷 주이동 1개·방향전환 최대 1회; 강한 컷도 원인 있는 이동만. 건축물 고정, 손과 접촉·발 위치 유지. 실제 이전 영상에서 마지막 사용 프레임과 관측 속도가 확보되는 경우에만 첫 0.3~0.5초 그 관성 유지. 현재는 실제 프레임 연결이 검증되지 않은 독립 이미지다. 물질 내부·지도·시대 전환은 편집/설명 화면 전환이며 실제 관통 촬영이 아니다.
```

Image prompt:

```text
LATEST USER CINEMATOGRAPHY REQUIREMENT: a composed live-action historical MOVIE still with shot-specific cinematic camera placement, NOT a fixed first-person game view, NOT a repeated behind-avatar game camera, no game HUD. Wide oblique aerial, low-angle architecture, telephoto layers, dynamic diagonal composition, intimate object close-ups and macro details alternate across the sequence. Do not automatically insert the viewer's arms or hands. Human fragments belong only when the stated story action needs them. No face-forward speaking presenter, talking head or frontal face close-up. Featured people are beautifully costumed but viewed from behind, cropped to shoulder/torso/hands, or hidden behind a motivated foreground element. Make spectacular surrounding architecture, fabric, flora and daily activity abundant yet compositionally controlled. A purposeful object, garment detail, horse, bird or other explicitly specified anchor may guide a later cinematic transition; never insert animals indiscriminately. Natural depth through camera placement, overlapping planes, visual scale and lighting. Use case: historical-scene (scientific and modern-research inserts as explicitly specified). Asset type: ONE independent full-frame cinematic documentary still, landscape 16:9, target 1536x864 or the closest supported high-quality landscape resolution. Never a collage, storyboard sheet, contact sheet, poster, screenshot, caption card or multiple panels. Premium photographic realism with tangible linen, rope, timber, skin, brick, stone and metal. Beautifully abundant surroundings organized into three readable depth layers; a strong single focal subject, a clear travel corridor and visible human scale when architectural. Color inspiration: jewel-like teal and emerald accents, cinnabar and terracotta, Egyptian blue, luminous ivory and controlled bronze-gold highlights, deep dimensional shadows with recoverable texture. Preserve historically plausible Roman architecture and dress; interpretive reconstruction rather than pretending to be an ancient photograph. No copied source-image people, watermarks, brands, interface, lettering, subtitles, fake inscriptions, fantasy armor, baroque rococo interiors, electric lights, modern cranes, Aurelian walls or Arch of Constantine in AD 117. No excessive bloom, uniformly orange grading, flat gray ruins, plastic faces or indiscriminate fog. Still image, no motion blur on the focal subject. Lens values are compositional intent, not claims of real camera metadata.
Shot 007. Scene: Cinematic side-on costume detail cropped strictly from an adult Roman residents shoulder to waist, textured linen sleeve, simple leather belt and a small bronze key, no head or face in frame. A rough doorway edge close foreground and a magnificent sunlit street beyond form three strong depth layers. This is an external camera insert, not the viewers looking down at their own body. 
Composition: INSERT CU 65mm 측면. Foreground: 팔. Midground: 소매와 허리띠. Background: 문턱 바닥. Hero subject: 거친 소매와 빈 허리띠, clearly readable near the center third. Depth comes from actual near/far camera placement, perspective, occlusion, scale and atmospheric separation, never merely digital zoom. warm morning sunlight from upper camera-left, cooler sky fill, bright edges and rich open shadows. Reserve a coherent central or near-central travel path. Image must stand alone with lush but purposeful surrounding detail.
```

별도 후속 영상화 제안 / 8초 편집 구간. 문턱에서 정지해 주소의 차이를 강조. 0~0.4초 시작 공간을 읽게 하고, 약 3.6초 주요 대상 도달, 6.0초 좁은 임대주택 문과 멀리 밝은 대저택 문이 한 골목에서 대비된다.의 정보를 읽게 함. 마지막 0.5초 다음 앵커 #013 수레바퀴 / CENTER_MIDDLE. 일반 컷 주이동 1개·방향전환 최대 1회; 강한 컷도 원인 있는 이동만. 건축물 고정, 손과 접촉·발 위치 유지. 실제 이전 영상에서 마지막 사용 프레임과 관측 속도가 확보되는 경우에만 첫 0.3~0.5초 그 관성 유지. 현재는 실제 프레임 연결이 검증되지 않은 독립 이미지다. 물질 내부·지도·시대 전환은 편집/설명 화면 전환이며 실제 관통 촬영이 아니다. 전환 요소 나뭇잎: 마지막 부분에서만 전경 가림으로 활용하고, 가림 뒤 다음 정보를 공개. 입자 발생원과 흐름 방향 고정.
```

Image prompt:

```text
LATEST USER CINEMATOGRAPHY REQUIREMENT: a composed live-action historical MOVIE still with shot-specific cinematic camera placement, NOT a fixed first-person game view, NOT a repeated behind-avatar game camera, no game HUD. Wide oblique aerial, low-angle architecture, telephoto layers, dynamic diagonal composition, intimate object close-ups and macro details alternate across the sequence. Do not automatically insert the viewer's arms or hands. Human fragments belong only when the stated story action needs them. No face-forward speaking presenter, talking head or frontal face close-up. Featured people are beautifully costumed but viewed from behind, cropped to shoulder/torso/hands, or hidden behind a motivated foreground element. Make spectacular surrounding architecture, fabric, flora and daily activity abundant yet compositionally controlled. A purposeful object, garment detail, horse, bird or other explicitly specified anchor may guide a later cinematic transition; never insert animals indiscriminately. Natural depth through camera placement, overlapping planes, visual scale and lighting. Use case: historical-scene (scientific and modern-research inserts as explicitly specified). Asset type: ONE independent full-frame cinematic documentary still, landscape 16:9, target 1536x864 or the closest supported high-quality landscape resolution. Never a collage, storyboard sheet, contact sheet, poster, screenshot, caption card or multiple panels. Premium photographic realism with tangible linen, rope, timber, skin, brick, stone and metal. Beautifully abundant surroundings organized into three readable depth layers; a strong single focal subject, a clear travel corridor and visible human scale when architectural. Color inspiration: jewel-like teal and emerald accents, cinnabar and terracotta, Egyptian blue, luminous ivory and controlled bronze-gold highlights, deep dimensional shadows with recoverable texture. Preserve historically plausible Roman architecture and dress; interpretive reconstruction rather than pretending to be an ancient photograph. No copied source-image people, watermarks, brands, interface, lettering, subtitles, fake inscriptions, fantasy armor, baroque rococo interiors, electric lights, modern cranes, Aurelian walls or Arch of Constantine in AD 117. No excessive bloom, uniformly orange grading, flat gray ruins, plastic faces or indiscriminate fog. Still image, no motion blur on the focal subject. Lens values are compositional intent, not claims of real camera metadata.
Shot 013. Scene: Ground-hugging view next to a Roman wooden delivery cart wheel at early morning, basalt street stones, an adult carrying bread passes behind, shopkeeper raises a wooden shutter, red and saffron awnings, deep street perspective. The muscular white foreleg and detailed leather harness of the cart horse enter only the far-left foreground edge as a physical speed and next-transition anchor; no full centered horse portrait.
Composition: GROUND 24mm. Foreground: 나무 바퀴. Midground: 빵 운반자. Background: 개점 거리. Hero subject: 수레바퀴, clearly readable near the center third. Depth comes from actual near/far camera placement, perspective, occlusion, scale and atmospheric separation, never merely digital zoom. directional Mediterranean sun from upper left with soft sky fill and controlled highlights. Reserve a coherent central or near-central travel path. Image must stand alone with lush but purposeful surrounding detail.
Optional physically motivated transition element: A small low puff of road dust comes only from the cart wheel and horse hoof, never a full-frame smoke cloud.
```

별도 후속 영상화 제안 / 4초 편집 구간. 통로 방향 전진으로 외부 지형 발견. 0~0.4초 시작 공간을 읽게 하고, 약 1.8초 주요 대상 도달, 3.0초 어두운 수로 내부 끝에서 밝은 계곡과 수로교가 나타난다.의 정보를 읽게 함. 마지막 0.5초 다음 앵커 #019 수로교 / CENTER_MIDDLE. 일반 컷 주이동 1개·방향전환 최대 1회; 강한 컷도 원인 있는 이동만. 건축물 고정, 손과 접촉·발 위치 유지. 실제 이전 영상에서 마지막 사용 프레임과 관측 속도가 확보되는 경우에만 첫 0.3~0.5초 그 관성 유지. 현재는 실제 프레임 연결이 검증되지 않은 독립 이미지다. 물질 내부·지도·시대 전환은 편집/설명 화면 전환이며 실제 관통 촬영이 아니다. 전환 요소 흐르는 수로의 물: 마지막 부분에서만 전경 가림으로 활용하고, 가림 뒤 다음 정보를 공개. 입자 발생원과 흐름 방향 고정.
```

Image prompt:

```text
LATEST USER CINEMATOGRAPHY REQUIREMENT: a composed live-action historical MOVIE still with shot-specific cinematic camera placement, NOT a fixed first-person game view, NOT a repeated behind-avatar game camera, no game HUD. Wide oblique aerial, low-angle architecture, telephoto layers, dynamic diagonal composition, intimate object close-ups and macro details alternate across the sequence. Do not automatically insert the viewer's arms or hands. Human fragments belong only when the stated story action needs them. No face-forward speaking presenter, talking head or frontal face close-up. Featured people are beautifully costumed but viewed from behind, cropped to shoulder/torso/hands, or hidden behind a motivated foreground element. Make spectacular surrounding architecture, fabric, flora and daily activity abundant yet compositionally controlled. A purposeful object, garment detail, horse, bird or other explicitly specified anchor may guide a later cinematic transition; never insert animals indiscriminately. Natural depth through camera placement, overlapping planes, visual scale and lighting. Use case: historical-scene (scientific and modern-research inserts as explicitly specified). Asset type: ONE independent full-frame cinematic documentary still, landscape 16:9, target 1536x864 or the closest supported high-quality landscape resolution. Never a collage, storyboard sheet, contact sheet, poster, screenshot, caption card or multiple panels. Premium photographic realism with tangible linen, rope, timber, skin, brick, stone and metal. Beautifully abundant surroundings organized into three readable depth layers; a strong single focal subject, a clear travel corridor and visible human scale when architectural. Color inspiration: jewel-like teal and emerald accents, cinnabar and terracotta, Egyptian blue, luminous ivory and controlled bronze-gold highlights, deep dimensional shadows with recoverable texture. Preserve historically plausible Roman architecture and dress; interpretive reconstruction rather than pretending to be an ancient photograph. No copied source-image people, watermarks, brands, interface, lettering, subtitles, fake inscriptions, fantasy armor, baroque rococo interiors, electric lights, modern cranes, Aurelian walls or Arch of Constantine in AD 117. No excessive bloom, uniformly orange grading, flat gray ruins, plastic faces or indiscriminate fog. Still image, no motion blur on the focal subject. Lens values are compositional intent, not claims of real camera metadata.
Shot 019. Scene: Sweeping aerial-oblique landscape of an ancient Roman aqueduct crossing a green valley, repeated stable stone arches, hills receding toward a distant lake region, tiny maintenance workers for scale, bright Mediterranean atmosphere, no impossible water going uphill. 
Composition: EW 24mm 사선 항공. Foreground: 수풀. Midground: 아치 수로교. Background: 산과 호수 방향. Hero subject: 수로교, clearly readable near the center third. Depth comes from actual near/far camera placement, perspective, occlusion, scale and atmospheric separation, never merely digital zoom. directional Mediterranean sun from upper left with soft sky fill and controlled highlights. Reserve a coherent central or near-central travel path. Image must stand alone with lush but purposeful surrounding detail.
Optional physically motivated transition element: Close foreground tree leaves frame one side of the aerial aqueduct reveal, with genuine bright sky gaps between the leaves.
```

Color inspiration: jewel-like teal and emerald accents, cinnabar and terracotta, Egyptian blue, luminous ivory and controlled bronze-gold highlights, deep dimensional shadows with recoverable texture. Preserve historically plausible Roman architecture and dress; interpretive reconstruction rather than pretending to be an ancient photograph. No copied source-image people, watermarks, brands, interface, lettering, subtitles, fake inscriptions, fantasy armor, baroque rococo interiors, electric lights, modern cranes, Aurelian walls or Arch of Constantine in AD 117. No excessive bloom, uniformly orange grading, flat gray ruins, plastic faces or indiscriminate fog. Still image, no motion blur on the focal subject. Lens values are compositional intent, not claims of real camera metadata.

Never a collage, storyboard sheet, contact sheet, poster, screenshot, caption card or multiple panels. Premium photographic realism with tangible linen, rope, timber, skin, brick, stone and metal. Beautifully abundant surroundings organized into three readable depth layers; a strong single focal subject, a clear travel corridor and visible human scale when architectural. Color inspiration: jewel-like teal and emerald accents, cinnabar and terracotta, Egyptian blue, luminous ivory and controlled bronze-gold highlights, deep dimensional shadows with recoverable texture. Preserve historically plausible Roman architecture and dress; interpretive reconstruction rather than pretending to be an ancient photograph. No copied source-image people, watermarks, brands, interface, lettering, subtitles, fake inscriptions, fantasy armor, baroque rococo interiors, electric lights, modern cranes, Aurelian walls or Arch of Constantine in AD 117. No excessive bloom, uniformly orange grading, flat gray ruins, plastic faces or indiscriminate fog. Still image, no motion blur on the focal subject. Lens values are compositional intent, not claims of real camera metadata.
Shot 028. Scene: Narrow dim top-floor corridor in a Roman insula, close rough doorframes, worn plank floor, tiny window at the end throws a precise shaft of daylight, warm dust motes only in that shaft, material detail retained in shadows.

Reserve a coherent central or near-central travel path. Image must stand alone with lush but purposeful surrounding detail.

Image prompt:

```text
LATEST USER CINEMATOGRAPHY REQUIREMENT: a composed live-action historical MOVIE still with shot-specific cinematic camera placement, NOT a fixed first-person game view, NOT a repeated behind-avatar game camera, no game HUD. Wide oblique aerial, low-angle architecture, telephoto layers, dynamic diagonal composition, intimate object close-ups and macro details alternate across the sequence. Do not automatically insert the viewer's arms or hands. Human fragments belong only when the stated story action needs them. No face-forward speaking presenter, talking head or frontal face close-up. Featured people are beautifully costumed but viewed from behind, cropped to shoulder/torso/hands, or hidden behind a motivated foreground element. Make spectacular surrounding architecture, fabric, flora and daily activity abundant yet compositionally controlled. A purposeful object, garment detail, horse, bird or other explicitly specified anchor may guide a later cinematic transition; never insert animals indiscriminately. Natural depth through camera placement, overlapping planes, visual scale and lighting. Use case: historical-scene (scientific and modern-research inserts as explicitly specified). Asset type: ONE independent full-frame cinematic documentary still, landscape 16:9, target 1536x864 or the closest supported high-quality landscape resolution. Never a collage, storyboard sheet, contact sheet, poster, screenshot, caption card or multiple panels. Premium photographic realism with tangible linen, rope, timber, skin, brick, stone and metal. Beautifully abundant surroundings organized into three readable depth layers; a strong single focal subject, a clear travel corridor and visible human scale when architectural. Color inspiration: jewel-like teal and emerald accents, cinnabar and terracotta, Egyptian blue, luminous ivory and controlled bronze-gold highlights, deep dimensional shadows with recoverable texture. Preserve historically plausible Roman architecture and dress; interpretive reconstruction rather than pretending to be an ancient photograph. No copied source-image people, watermarks, brands, interface, lettering, subtitles, fake inscriptions, fantasy armor, baroque rococo interiors, electric lights, modern cranes, Aurelian walls or Arch of Constantine in AD 117. No excessive bloom, uniformly orange grading, flat gray ruins, plastic faces or indiscriminate fog. Still image, no motion blur on the focal subject. Lens values are compositional intent, not claims of real camera metadata.
Shot 031. Scene: Over the back of a seated Roman satirist interpreted as Juvenal, only rear shoulder, forearms and hands holding a papyrus roll visible. Camera looks past him into a cramped rented room with an oil lamp, timber and rough plaster. Face fully outside frame, no readable fabricated quotation. 
Composition: REAR OTS 50mm. Foreground: 벌어진 석고. Midground: 들보와 층. Background: 지붕. Hero subject: 두루마리, clearly readable near the center third. Depth comes from actual near/far camera placement, perspective, occlusion, scale and atmospheric separation, never merely digital zoom. neutral educational raking light from upper left, legible physical construction layers. Reserve a coherent central or near-central travel path. Image must stand alone with lush but purposeful surrounding detail.
```

Image prompt:

```text
LATEST USER CINEMATOGRAPHY REQUIREMENT: a composed live-action historical MOVIE still with shot-specific cinematic camera placement, NOT a fixed first-person game view, NOT a repeated behind-avatar game camera, no game HUD. Wide oblique aerial, low-angle architecture, telephoto layers, dynamic diagonal composition, intimate object close-ups and macro details alternate across the sequence. Do not automatically insert the viewer's arms or hands. Human fragments belong only when the stated story action needs them. No face-forward speaking presenter, talking head or frontal face close-up. Featured people are beautifully costumed but viewed from behind, cropped to shoulder/torso/hands, or hidden behind a motivated foreground element. Make spectacular surrounding architecture, fabric, flora and daily activity abundant yet compositionally controlled. A purposeful object, garment detail, horse, bird or other explicitly specified anchor may guide a later cinematic transition; never insert animals indiscriminately. Natural depth through camera placement, overlapping planes, visual scale and lighting. Use case: historical-scene (scientific and modern-research inserts as explicitly specified). Asset type: ONE independent full-frame cinematic documentary still, landscape 16:9, target 1536x864 or the closest supported high-quality landscape resolution. Never a collage, storyboard sheet, contact sheet, poster, screenshot, caption card or multiple panels. Premium photographic realism with tangible linen, rope, timber, skin, brick, stone and metal. Beautifully abundant surroundings organized into three readable depth layers; a strong single focal subject, a clear travel corridor and visible human scale when architectural. Color inspiration: jewel-like teal and emerald accents, cinnabar and terracotta, Egyptian blue, luminous ivory and controlled bronze-gold highlights, deep dimensional shadows with recoverable texture. Preserve historically plausible Roman architecture and dress; interpretive reconstruction rather than pretending to be an ancient photograph. No copied source-image people, watermarks, brands, interface, lettering, subtitles, fake inscriptions, fantasy armor, baroque rococo interiors, electric lights, modern cranes, Aurelian walls or Arch of Constantine in AD 117. No excessive bloom, uniformly orange grading, flat gray ruins, plastic faces or indiscriminate fog. Still image, no motion blur on the focal subject. Lens values are compositional intent, not claims of real camera metadata.
Shot 034. Scene: View from a poor Roman rental room through a narrow window onto a lively street of workshops, hiring laborers and merchants, rough interior foreground and bright economic activity outside, visual answer to why people remain in the city. 
Composition: POV 50mm. Foreground: 거친 창틀. Midground: 공방들. Background: 큰 도로. Hero subject: 일터의 거리, clearly readable near the center third. Depth comes from actual near/far camera placement, perspective, occlusion, scale and atmospheric separation, never merely digital zoom. directional Mediterranean sun from upper left with soft sky fill and controlled highlights. Reserve a coherent central or near-central travel path. Image must stand alone with lush but purposeful surrounding detail.
```

별도 후속 영상화 제안 / 5초 편집 구간. 가벼운 전진으로 음식의 질감 발견. 0~0.4초 시작 공간을 읽게 하고, 약 2.2초 주요 대상 도달, 3.8초 갓 구운 빵과 올리브가 놓인 시장 좌판 가까이 다가간다.의 정보를 읽게 함. 마지막 0.5초 다음 앵커 #037 가룸 항아리 / CENTER_MIDDLE. 일반 컷 주이동 1개·방향전환 최대 1회; 강한 컷도 원인 있는 이동만. 건축물 고정, 손과 접촉·발 위치 유지. 실제 이전 영상에서 마지막 사용 프레임과 관측 속도가 확보되는 경우에만 첫 0.3~0.5초 그 관성 유지. 현재는 실제 프레임 연결이 검증되지 않은 독립 이미지다. 물질 내부·지도·시대 전환은 편집/설명 화면 전환이며 실제 관통 촬영이 아니다. 전환 요소 오븐의 따뜻한 김: 마지막 부분에서만 전경 가림으로 활용하고, 가림 뒤 다음 정보를 공개. 입자 발생원과 흐름 방향 고정.
```

Image prompt:

```text
LATEST USER CINEMATOGRAPHY REQUIREMENT: a composed live-action historical MOVIE still with shot-specific cinematic camera placement, NOT a fixed first-person game view, NOT a repeated behind-avatar game camera, no game HUD. Wide oblique aerial, low-angle architecture, telephoto layers, dynamic diagonal composition, intimate object close-ups and macro details alternate across the sequence. Do not automatically insert the viewer's arms or hands. Human fragments belong only when the stated story action needs them. No face-forward speaking presenter, talking head or frontal face close-up. Featured people are beautifully costumed but viewed from behind, cropped to shoulder/torso/hands, or hidden behind a motivated foreground element. Make spectacular surrounding architecture, fabric, flora and daily activity abundant yet compositionally controlled. A purposeful object, garment detail, horse, bird or other explicitly specified anchor may guide a later cinematic transition; never insert animals indiscriminately. Natural depth through camera placement, overlapping planes, visual scale and lighting. Use case: historical-scene (scientific and modern-research inserts as explicitly specified). Asset type: ONE independent full-frame cinematic documentary still, landscape 16:9, target 1536x864 or the closest supported high-quality landscape resolution. Never a collage, storyboard sheet, contact sheet, poster, screenshot, caption card or multiple panels. Premium photographic realism with tangible linen, rope, timber, skin, brick, stone and metal. Beautifully abundant surroundings organized into three readable depth layers; a strong single focal subject, a clear travel corridor and visible human scale when architectural. Color inspiration: jewel-like teal and emerald accents, cinnabar and terracotta, Egyptian blue, luminous ivory and controlled bronze-gold highlights, deep dimensional shadows with recoverable texture. Preserve historically plausible Roman architecture and dress; interpretive reconstruction rather than pretending to be an ancient photograph. No copied source-image people, watermarks, brands, interface, lettering, subtitles, fake inscriptions, fantasy armor, baroque rococo interiors, electric lights, modern cranes, Aurelian walls or Arch of Constantine in AD 117. No excessive bloom, uniformly orange grading, flat gray ruins, plastic faces or indiscriminate fog. Still image, no motion blur on the focal subject. Lens values are compositional intent, not claims of real camera metadata.
Shot 037. Scene: Top-down Roman market still life with dried fish, figs, grapes, simple wine vessels and a small garum amphora, colorful but believable ceramic and woven baskets, no luxury modern crystal, deeply tactile surfaces and sunlight stripes. 
Composition: TOP 35mm. Foreground: 항아리 테두리. Midground: 식재료. Background: 좌판 천. Hero subject: 가룸 항아리, clearly readable near the center third. Depth comes from actual near/far camera placement, perspective, occlusion, scale and atmospheric separation, never merely digital zoom. warm sunlight filtered by linen awnings, rich food color and cool dimensional shade. Reserve a coherent central or near-central travel path. Image must stand alone with lush but purposeful surrounding detail.
```

Image prompt:

```text
LATEST USER CINEMATOGRAPHY REQUIREMENT: a composed live-action historical MOVIE still with shot-specific cinematic camera placement, NOT a fixed first-person game view, NOT a repeated behind-avatar game camera, no game HUD. Wide oblique aerial, low-angle architecture, telephoto layers, dynamic diagonal composition, intimate object close-ups and macro details alternate across the sequence. Do not automatically insert the viewer's arms or hands. Human fragments belong only when the stated story action needs them. No face-forward speaking presenter, talking head or frontal face close-up. Featured people are beautifully costumed but viewed from behind, cropped to shoulder/torso/hands, or hidden behind a motivated foreground element. Make spectacular surrounding architecture, fabric, flora and daily activity abundant yet compositionally controlled. A purposeful object, garment detail, horse, bird or other explicitly specified anchor may guide a later cinematic transition; never insert animals indiscriminately. Natural depth through camera placement, overlapping planes, visual scale and lighting. Use case: historical-scene (scientific and modern-research inserts as explicitly specified). Asset type: ONE independent full-frame cinematic documentary still, landscape 16:9, target 1536x864 or the closest supported high-quality landscape resolution. Never a collage, storyboard sheet, contact sheet, poster, screenshot, caption card or multiple panels. Premium photographic realism with tangible linen, rope, timber, skin, brick, stone and metal. Beautifully abundant surroundings organized into three readable depth layers; a strong single focal subject, a clear travel corridor and visible human scale when architectural. Color inspiration: jewel-like teal and emerald accents, cinnabar and terracotta, Egyptian blue, luminous ivory and controlled bronze-gold highlights, deep dimensional shadows with recoverable texture. Preserve historically plausible Roman architecture and dress; interpretive reconstruction rather than pretending to be an ancient photograph. No copied source-image people, watermarks, brands, interface, lettering, subtitles, fake inscriptions, fantasy armor, baroque rococo interiors, electric lights, modern cranes, Aurelian walls or Arch of Constantine in AD 117. No excessive bloom, uniformly orange grading, flat gray ruins, plastic faces or indiscriminate fog. Still image, no motion blur on the focal subject. Lens values are compositional intent, not claims of real camera metadata.
Shot 040. Scene: Low medium-wide view in a Roman grain warehouse, porters lift a single heavy grain sack from an organized stack onto a wooden cart, massive brick storage bays receding behind, clear path, detailed rope fibers and dusty plaster. 
Composition: LOW MED 28mm. Foreground: 자루. Midground: 운반자와 수레. Background: 창고 회랑. Hero subject: 곡물 자루, clearly readable near the center third. Depth comes from actual near/far camera placement, perspective, occlusion, scale and atmospheric separation, never merely digital zoom. golden side-back sun, teal water bounce, wet hull texture and restrained specular highlights. Reserve a coherent central or near-central travel path. Image must stand alone with lush but purposeful surrounding detail.
```

Wide oblique aerial, low-angle architecture, telephoto layers, dynamic diagonal composition, intimate object close-ups and macro details alternate across the sequence. Featured people are beautifully costumed but viewed from behind, cropped to shoulder/torso/hands, or hidden behind a motivated foreground element. Make spectacular surrounding architecture, fabric, flora and daily activity abundant yet compositionally controlled. A purposeful object, garment detail, horse, bird or other explicitly specified anchor may guide a later cinematic transition; never insert animals indiscriminately. Natural depth through camera placement, overlapping planes, visual scale and lighting. Use case: historical-scene (scientific and modern-research inserts as explicitly specified). Asset type: ONE independent full-frame cinematic documentary still, landscape 16:9, target 1536x864 or the closest supported high-quality landscape resolution. Never a collage, storyboard sheet, contact sheet, poster, screenshot, caption card or multiple panels. Premium photographic realism with tangible linen, rope, timber, skin, brick, stone and metal. Beautifully abundant surroundings organized into three readable depth layers; a strong single focal subject, a clear travel corridor and visible human scale when architectural. Color inspiration: jewel-like teal and emerald accents, cinnabar and terracotta, Egyptian blue, luminous ivory and controlled bronze-gold highlights, deep dimensional shadows with recoverable texture. Preserve historically plausible Roman architecture and dress; interpretive reconstruction rather than pretending to be an ancient photograph. No copied source-image people, watermarks, brands, interface, lettering, subtitles, fake inscriptions, fantasy armor, baroque rococo interiors, electric lights, modern cranes, Aurelian walls or Arch of Constantine in AD 117. No excessive bloom, uniformly orange grading, flat gray ruins, plastic faces or indiscriminate fog. Still image, no motion blur on the focal subject. Lens values are compositional intent, not claims of real camera metadata.
Shot 043. Scene: High wide view of grain wagons and pack animals entering a densely populated Roman district along a broad road, warehouses at near edge and layered rooftops beyond, realistic number of foreground vehicles with distant flow, no anachronistic late city wall. 
Composition: HIGH WIDE 40mm. Foreground: 곡물 자루. Midground: 유입 수레. Background: 로마 지붕. Hero subject: 곡물 수레 행렬, clearly readable near the center third. Depth comes from actual near/far camera placement, perspective, occlusion, scale and atmospheric separation, never merely digital zoom. directional Mediterranean sun from upper left with soft sky fill and controlled highlights. Reserve a coherent central or near-central travel path. Image must stand alone with lush but purposeful surrounding detail.
```

Composition: AXIAL EW 18mm. Foreground: 출입 주랑. Midground: 기마상. Background: 바실리카 울피아. Hero subject: 포룸 광장, clearly readable near the center third. Depth comes from actual near/far camera placement, perspective, occlusion, scale and atmospheric separation, never merely digital zoom. warm upper-left sunlight, luminous colored marble, bronze highlights, deep cool portico shadows. Reserve a coherent central or near-central travel path. Image must stand alone with lush but purposeful surrounding detail.
Optional physically motivated transition element: A near hanging textile and a small courtyard leaf branch can briefly occlude a later moving camera, with a clear view beyond toward the forum.

```text
LATEST USER CINEMATOGRAPHY REQUIREMENT: a composed live-action historical MOVIE still with shot-specific cinematic camera placement, NOT a fixed first-person game view, NOT a repeated behind-avatar game camera, no game HUD. Wide oblique aerial, low-angle architecture, telephoto layers, dynamic diagonal composition, intimate object close-ups and macro details alternate across the sequence. Do not automatically insert the viewer's arms or hands. Human fragments belong only when the stated story action needs them. No face-forward speaking presenter, talking head or frontal face close-up. Featured people are beautifully costumed but viewed from behind, cropped to shoulder/torso/hands, or hidden behind a motivated foreground element. Make spectacular surrounding architecture, fabric, flora and daily activity abundant yet compositionally controlled. A purposeful object, garment detail, horse, bird or other explicitly specified anchor may guide a later cinematic transition; never insert animals indiscriminately. Natural depth through camera placement, overlapping planes, visual scale and lighting. Use case: historical-scene (scientific and modern-research inserts as explicitly specified). Asset type: ONE independent full-frame cinematic documentary still, landscape 16:9, target 1536x864 or the closest supported high-quality landscape resolution. Never a collage, storyboard sheet, contact sheet, poster, screenshot, caption card or multiple panels. Premium photographic realism with tangible linen, rope, timber, skin, brick, stone and metal. Beautifully abundant surroundings organized into three readable depth layers; a strong single focal subject, a clear travel corridor and visible human scale when architectural. Color inspiration: jewel-like teal and emerald accents, cinnabar and terracotta, Egyptian blue, luminous ivory and controlled bronze-gold highlights, deep dimensional shadows with recoverable texture. Preserve historically plausible Roman architecture and dress; interpretive reconstruction rather than pretending to be an ancient photograph. No copied source-image people, watermarks, brands, interface, lettering, subtitles, fake inscriptions, fantasy armor, baroque rococo interiors, electric lights, modern cranes, Aurelian walls or Arch of Constantine in AD 117. No excessive bloom, uniformly orange grading, flat gray ruins, plastic faces or indiscriminate fog. Still image, no motion blur on the focal subject. Lens values are compositional intent, not claims of real camera metadata.
Shot 049. Scene: Floor-level architectural photograph inside Basilica Ulpia, richly veined imported colored marble slabs foreground, towering ordered column rows, coffered timber roof above, sober Roman imperial geometry, polished highlights, no church nave cross or baroque ornament. 
Composition: FLOOR LOW 21mm. Foreground: 다색 석재. Midground: 기둥. Background: 바실리카 천장. Hero subject: 수입 대리석, clearly readable near the center third. Depth comes from actual near/far camera placement, perspective, occlusion, scale and atmospheric separation, never merely digital zoom. warm upper-left sunlight, luminous colored marble, bronze highlights, deep cool portico shadows. Reserve a coherent central or near-central travel path. Image must stand alone with lush but purposeful surrounding detail.

A purposeful object, garment detail, horse, bird or other explicitly specified anchor may guide a later cinematic transition; never insert animals indiscriminately. Natural depth through camera placement, overlapping planes, visual scale and lighting. Use case: historical-scene (scientific and modern-research inserts as explicitly specified). Asset type: ONE independent full-frame cinematic documentary still, landscape 16:9, target 1536x864 or the closest supported high-quality landscape resolution. Never a collage, storyboard sheet, contact sheet, poster, screenshot, caption card or multiple panels. Premium photographic realism with tangible linen, rope, timber, skin, brick, stone and metal. Beautifully abundant surroundings organized into three readable depth layers; a strong single focal subject, a clear travel corridor and visible human scale when architectural. Color inspiration: jewel-like teal and emerald accents, cinnabar and terracotta, Egyptian blue, luminous ivory and controlled bronze-gold highlights, deep dimensional shadows with recoverable texture. Preserve historically plausible Roman architecture and dress; interpretive reconstruction rather than pretending to be an ancient photograph. No copied source-image people, watermarks, brands, interface, lettering, subtitles, fake inscriptions, fantasy armor, baroque rococo interiors, electric lights, modern cranes, Aurelian walls or Arch of Constantine in AD 117. No excessive bloom, uniformly orange grading, flat gray ruins, plastic faces or indiscriminate fog. Still image, no motion blur on the focal subject. Lens values are compositional intent, not claims of real camera metadata.

Depth comes from actual near/far camera placement, perspective, occlusion, scale and atmospheric separation, never merely digital zoom. cool mountainous daylight with warm side-back sun, worn metal reflections and red wool accents. Reserve a coherent central or near-central travel path. Image must stand alone with lush but purposeful surrounding detail.
Optional physically motivated transition element: Thin ground-hugging dust from advancing footsteps can provide a near-edge wipe; no fire, explosion or colored smoke.

LATEST USER CINEMATOGRAPHY REQUIREMENT: a composed live-action historical MOVIE still with shot-specific cinematic camera placement, NOT a fixed first-person game view, NOT a repeated behind-avatar game camera, no game HUD. Wide oblique aerial, low-angle architecture, telephoto layers, dynamic diagonal composition, intimate object close-ups and macro details alternate across the sequence. Do not automatically insert the viewer's arms or hands. Human fragments belong only when the stated story action needs them. No face-forward speaking presenter, talking head or frontal face close-up. Featured people are beautifully costumed but viewed from behind, cropped to shoulder/torso/hands, or hidden behind a motivated foreground element. Make spectacular surrounding architecture, fabric, flora and daily activity abundant yet compositionally controlled. A purposeful object, garment detail, horse, bird or other explicitly specified anchor may guide a later cinematic transition; never insert animals indiscriminately. Natural depth through camera placement, overlapping planes, visual scale and lighting. Use case: historical-scene (scientific and modern-research inserts as explicitly specified). Asset type: ONE independent full-frame cinematic documentary still, landscape 16:9, target 1536x864 or the closest supported high-quality landscape resolution. Never a collage, storyboard sheet, contact sheet, poster, screenshot, caption card or multiple panels. Premium photographic realism with tangible linen, rope, timber, skin, brick, stone and metal. Beautifully abundant surroundings organized into three readable depth layers; a strong single focal subject, a clear travel corridor and visible human scale when architectural. Color inspiration: jewel-like teal and emerald accents, cinnabar and terracotta, Egyptian blue, luminous ivory and controlled bronze-gold highlights, deep dimensional shadows with recoverable texture. Preserve historically plausible Roman architecture and dress; interpretive reconstruction rather than pretending to be an ancient photograph. No copied source-image people, watermarks, brands, interface, lettering, subtitles, fake inscriptions, fantasy armor, baroque rococo interiors, electric lights, modern cranes, Aurelian walls or Arch of Constantine in AD 117. No excessive bloom, uniformly orange grading, flat gray ruins, plastic faces or indiscriminate fog. Still image, no motion blur on the focal subject. Lens values are compositional intent, not claims of real camera metadata.
Shot 063. Scene: Extreme macro archaeological view of a Roman stamped brick fragment, real baked red clay grit, shallow circular maker's stamp partly worn and not falsely readable, adult fingertips at edge for scale, raking natural light, background workbench. 
Composition: MACRO 100mm. Foreground: 붉은 점토 입자. Midground: 희미한 도장. Background: 작업대. Hero subject: 벽돌 도장, clearly readable near the center third. Depth comes from actual near/far camera placement, perspective, occlusion, scale and atmospheric separation, never merely digital zoom. warm afternoon sun grazing brick and timber, soft sky fill, tiny motivated construction dust. Reserve a coherent central or near-central travel path. Image must stand alone with lush but purposeful surrounding detail.
```

별도 후속 영상화 제안 / 5초 편집 구간. 사광과 초점 이동으로 도장 형상 발견. 0~0.4초 시작 공간을 읽게 하고, 약 2.2초 주요 대상 도달, 3.8초 흙 묻은 벽돌 표면의 제작 도장을 극근접으로 확인한다.의 정보를 읽게 함. 마지막 0.5초 다음 앵커 #064 비교용 벽돌 표본 / CENTER_MIDDLE. 일반 컷 주이동 1개·방향전환 최대 1회; 강한 컷도 원인 있는 이동만. 건축물 고정, 손과 접촉·발 위치 유지. 실제 이전 영상에서 마지막 사용 프레임과 관측 속도가 확보되는 경우에만 첫 0.3~0.5초 그 관성 유지. 현재는 실제 프레임 연결이 검증되지 않은 독립 이미지다. 물질 내부·지도·시대 전환은 편집/설명 화면 전환이며 실제 관통 촬영이 아니다.
```

A purposeful object, garment detail, horse, bird or other explicitly specified anchor may guide a later cinematic transition; never insert animals indiscriminately. Natural depth through camera placement, overlapping planes, visual scale and lighting. Use case: historical-scene (scientific and modern-research inserts as explicitly specified). Asset type: ONE independent full-frame cinematic documentary still, landscape 16:9, target 1536x864 or the closest supported high-quality landscape resolution. Never a collage, storyboard sheet, contact sheet, poster, screenshot, caption card or multiple panels. Premium photographic realism with tangible linen, rope, timber, skin, brick, stone and metal. Beautifully abundant surroundings organized into three readable depth layers; a strong single focal subject, a clear travel corridor and visible human scale when architectural. Color inspiration: jewel-like teal and emerald accents, cinnabar and terracotta, Egyptian blue, luminous ivory and controlled bronze-gold highlights, deep dimensional shadows with recoverable texture. Preserve historically plausible Roman architecture and dress; interpretive reconstruction rather than pretending to be an ancient photograph. No copied source-image people, watermarks, brands, interface, lettering, subtitles, fake inscriptions, fantasy armor, baroque rococo interiors, electric lights, modern cranes, Aurelian walls or Arch of Constantine in AD 117. No excessive bloom, uniformly orange grading, flat gray ruins, plastic faces or indiscriminate fog. Still image, no motion blur on the focal subject. Lens values are compositional intent, not claims of real camera metadata.
Shot 075. Scene: Close luxury detail of an ancient Roman translucent blue-green blown glass cup held by a clothed adult beside intensely colored fresco, subtle air bubbles and thin glass rim, warm skin, deep red and lapis blue background, no modern faceted gemstone goblet. 
Composition: CU 65mm. Foreground: 청록 유리. Midground: 성인 손. Background: 채색 벽화. Hero subject: 로마 유리잔, clearly readable near the center third. Depth comes from actual near/far camera placement, perspective, occlusion, scale and atmospheric separation, never merely digital zoom. luminous atrium daylight from upper left, rich cinnabar and blue frescoes, subtle gold and glass reflections. Reserve a coherent central or near-central travel path. Image must stand alone with lush but purposeful surrounding detail.

LATEST USER CINEMATOGRAPHY REQUIREMENT: a composed live-action historical MOVIE still with shot-specific cinematic camera placement, NOT a fixed first-person game view, NOT a repeated behind-avatar game camera, no game HUD. A purposeful object, garment detail, horse, bird or other explicitly specified anchor may guide a later cinematic transition; never insert animals indiscriminately. Natural depth through camera placement, overlapping planes, visual scale and lighting. Use case: historical-scene (scientific and modern-research inserts as explicitly specified). Asset type: ONE independent full-frame cinematic documentary still, landscape 16:9, target 1536x864 or the closest supported high-quality landscape resolution. Never a collage, storyboard sheet, contact sheet, poster, screenshot, caption card or multiple panels. Premium photographic realism with tangible linen, rope, timber, skin, brick, stone and metal. Beautifully abundant surroundings organized into three readable depth layers; a strong single focal subject, a clear travel corridor and visible human scale when architectural. Color inspiration: jewel-like teal and emerald accents, cinnabar and terracotta, Egyptian blue, luminous ivory and controlled bronze-gold highlights, deep dimensional shadows with recoverable texture. Preserve historically plausible Roman architecture and dress; interpretive reconstruction rather than pretending to be an ancient photograph. No copied source-image people, watermarks, brands, interface, lettering, subtitles, fake inscriptions, fantasy armor, baroque rococo interiors, electric lights, modern cranes, Aurelian walls or Arch of Constantine in AD 117. No excessive bloom, uniformly orange grading, flat gray ruins, plastic faces or indiscriminate fog. Still image, no motion blur on the focal subject. Lens values are compositional intent, not claims of real camera metadata.
Shot 081. Scene: Cramped top-floor Roman insula room at hot dusk, one plain bed, rough plaster and low timber roof, a small window admitting warm last light, a simple oil lamp not yet dominant, residents' ordinary possessions, honest dignity and spatial confinement. 
Composition: WIDE 28mm. Foreground: 거친 침상. Midground: 작은 방. Background: 어두워지는 창. Hero subject: 작은 방의 창, clearly readable near the center third. Depth comes from actual near/far camera placement, perspective, occlusion, scale and atmospheric separation, never merely digital zoom. low golden evening sun and sparse warm practical oil lamps against deep blue ambient shadows. Reserve a coherent central or near-central travel path. Image must stand alone with lush but purposeful surrounding detail.

Wide oblique aerial, low-angle architecture, telephoto layers, dynamic diagonal composition, intimate object close-ups and macro details alternate across the sequence. Featured people are beautifully costumed but viewed from behind, cropped to shoulder/torso/hands, or hidden behind a motivated foreground element. Make spectacular surrounding architecture, fabric, flora and daily activity abundant yet compositionally controlled. A purposeful object, garment detail, horse, bird or other explicitly specified anchor may guide a later cinematic transition; never insert animals indiscriminately. Natural depth through camera placement, overlapping planes, visual scale and lighting. Use case: historical-scene (scientific and modern-research inserts as explicitly specified). Asset type: ONE independent full-frame cinematic documentary still, landscape 16:9, target 1536x864 or the closest supported high-quality landscape resolution. Never a collage, storyboard sheet, contact sheet, poster, screenshot, caption card or multiple panels. Premium photographic realism with tangible linen, rope, timber, skin, brick, stone and metal. Beautifully abundant surroundings organized into three readable depth layers; a strong single focal subject, a clear travel corridor and visible human scale when architectural. Color inspiration: jewel-like teal and emerald accents, cinnabar and terracotta, Egyptian blue, luminous ivory and controlled bronze-gold highlights, deep dimensional shadows with recoverable texture. Preserve historically plausible Roman architecture and dress; interpretive reconstruction rather than pretending to be an ancient photograph. No copied source-image people, watermarks, brands, interface, lettering, subtitles, fake inscriptions, fantasy armor, baroque rococo interiors, electric lights, modern cranes, Aurelian walls or Arch of Constantine in AD 117. No excessive bloom, uniformly orange grading, flat gray ruins, plastic faces or indiscriminate fog. Still image, no motion blur on the focal subject. Lens values are compositional intent, not claims of real camera metadata.
Shot 084. Scene: Cinematic side-rear view of a Roman messenger cropped from shoulders down as he opens a dispatch at a plaza, only the messenger hands belong to the action. Behind him a crowd seen mostly from backs pauses, dusk torchlight reveals clothing and spatial depth, no visible frontal face, no viewers arms. 
Composition: SIDE OTS MED 50mm. Foreground: 문서를 든 손. Midground: 돌아선 군중. Background: 광장. Hero subject: 봉인 문서, clearly readable near the center third. Depth comes from actual near/far camera placement, perspective, occlusion, scale and atmospheric separation, never merely digital zoom. low golden evening sun and sparse warm practical oil lamps against deep blue ambient shadows. Reserve a coherent central or near-central travel path. Image must stand alone with lush but purposeful surrounding detail.

LATEST USER CINEMATOGRAPHY REQUIREMENT: a composed live-action historical MOVIE still with shot-specific cinematic camera placement, NOT a fixed first-person game view, NOT a repeated behind-avatar game camera, no game HUD. Wide oblique aerial, low-angle architecture, telephoto layers, dynamic diagonal composition, intimate object close-ups and macro details alternate across the sequence. Do not automatically insert the viewer's arms or hands. Human fragments belong only when the stated story action needs them. No face-forward speaking presenter, talking head or frontal face close-up. Featured people are beautifully costumed but viewed from behind, cropped to shoulder/torso/hands, or hidden behind a motivated foreground element. Make spectacular surrounding architecture, fabric, flora and daily activity abundant yet compositionally controlled. A purposeful object, garment detail, horse, bird or other explicitly specified anchor may guide a later cinematic transition; never insert animals indiscriminately. Natural depth through camera placement, overlapping planes, visual scale and lighting. Use case: historical-scene (scientific and modern-research inserts as explicitly specified). Asset type: ONE independent full-frame cinematic documentary still, landscape 16:9, target 1536x864 or the closest supported high-quality landscape resolution. Never a collage, storyboard sheet, contact sheet, poster, screenshot, caption card or multiple panels. Premium photographic realism with tangible linen, rope, timber, skin, brick, stone and metal. Beautifully abundant surroundings organized into three readable depth layers; a strong single focal subject, a clear travel corridor and visible human scale when architectural. Color inspiration: jewel-like teal and emerald accents, cinnabar and terracotta, Egyptian blue, luminous ivory and controlled bronze-gold highlights, deep dimensional shadows with recoverable texture. Preserve historically plausible Roman architecture and dress; interpretive reconstruction rather than pretending to be an ancient photograph. No copied source-image people, watermarks, brands, interface, lettering, subtitles, fake inscriptions, fantasy armor, baroque rococo interiors, electric lights, modern cranes, Aurelian walls or Arch of Constantine in AD 117. No excessive bloom, uniformly orange grading, flat gray ruins, plastic faces or indiscriminate fog. Still image, no motion blur on the focal subject. Lens values are compositional intent, not claims of real camera metadata.
Shot 087. Scene: Oblique wide educational terrain map spanning Britain to Mesopotamia with Mediterranean central, restrained small Roman standard markers and a subtle conceptual route line, distant eastern terrain fading into atmosphere, emphasis on geographic distance, no city names or exact territorial boundary text. 
Composition: MAP OBLIQUE 24mm. Foreground: 서부 지형. Midground: 지중해. Background: 메소포타미아. Hero subject: 동쪽 국경, clearly readable near the center third. Depth comes from actual near/far camera placement, perspective, occlusion, scale and atmospheric separation, never merely digital zoom. cinematic relief-map lighting, navy water and warm terrain, restrained brass routes. Reserve a coherent central or near-central travel path. Image must stand alone with lush but purposeful surrounding detail.
```

```
